# Agents · A · part 18

1,025 entries, architect to architect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **architect**: The squad's architect. Turns a feature idea or product brief into a complete, implementable design spec. Activate when the user asks to specify, plan, or document a new feature before implementation. Produces &lt;specs-dir&gt;/YYYY-MM-DD-feature/spec.md with overview, scope, user flow, architecture, and API contracts — but NO implementation tasks (that is the planner's job). Stack-agnostic: reads the project profile at runtime. ([gustavobaptista/specops](https://github.com/gustavobaptista/specops/blob/HEAD/agents/architect.md) · ★1)
- **architect**: Designs the smallest correct architecture inside the host app - boundaries, data model, contracts, authorization, consistency, failure, rollout - with ADRs and thin vertical slices. Use in the Plan phase. ([hacagahasanli/agents-know](https://github.com/hacagahasanli/agents-know/blob/HEAD/agents/architect.md) · ★1)
- **architect**: **Setup:** PRD for a shared task board in a multi-tenant host app: tasks have a position in a column, a counter of open tasks per board, and real-time updates to everyone viewing the board. The host already has a WebSocket channel and a `workspace_id` on every table. ([hacagahasanli/agents-know](https://github.com/hacagahasanli/agents-know/blob/HEAD/evals/agents/architect.md) · ★1)

This includes questions about how services should integrate, impact analysis for API changes, designing new services, evaluating architectural patterns, identifying dependencies, or making technology decisions that span multiple services. - **architect**: Frontier consultant the lead calls for a judgement call mid-task — a result it cannot explain, a rule or bar to set before measuring, a design choice with real trade-offs, a root cause after two failed fixes. Read-only; answers a question, does not write plans (use planner) or code. Resume the same architect with SendMessage for follow-ups in one thread of work.
